# RTYLR Role and Access Control V2 feature matrix

Reviewed from `packages/types/src/role-access.ts::KNOWN_PERMISSION_KEYS` on 2026-08-27. Backend entries identify concrete handler or service boundaries and frontend entries identify the route or component gate. The five Menu records below are classified as public product behavior for customer-facing browsing and ordering, so those routes are outside the employee RBAC gate. The corresponding internal management endpoints remain protected by the listed Menu permissions in Dash. The complete permission and lifecycle probe is `internal/platform/authorization/feature_matrix_test.go`.

The machine-readable source is [role-access-control-v2-feature-matrix.json](./role-access-control-v2-feature-matrix.json).
